51:## How cross-model workflow actually looks in production
53:![Cross-Model Workflow](assets/cross-model-workflow.png)

1:# Claude Memory
3:Persistent context via CLAUDE.md files — how to write them and how they load in monorepos.
14:## 1. Writing a Good CLAUDE.md
16:A well-structured CLAUDE.md is the single most impactful way to improve Claude Code's output for your project. Humanlayer has an excellent guide covering what to include, how to structure it, and common pitfalls.
22:## 2. CLAUDE.md in Large Monorepos
24:When working with Claude Code in a monorepo, understanding how CLAUDE.md files are loaded into context is crucial for organizing your project instructions effectively.
27:  <a href="https://x.com/bcherny/status/2016339448863355206"><img src="assets/claude-memory/claude-memory-monorepo.jpg" alt="CLAUDE.md loading in monorepos" width="600"></a>
32:Claude Code uses two distinct mechanisms for loading CLAUDE.md files:
36:When you start Claude Code, it walks **upward** from your current working directory toward the filesystem root and loads every CLAUDE.md it finds along the way. These files are loaded **immediately at startup**.
40:CLAUDE.md files in subdirectories below your current working directory are **NOT loaded at launch**. They are only included when Claude reads files in those subdirectories during your session. This is known as **lazy loading**.
48:├── CLAUDE.md          # Root-level instructions (shared across all components)
50:│   └── CLAUDE.md      # Frontend-specific instructions
52:│   └── CLAUDE.md      # Backend-specific instructions
54:    └── CLAUDE.md      # API-specific instructions
68:| `/mymonorepo/CLAUDE.md` | Yes | It's your current working directory |
69:| `/mymonorepo/frontend/CLAUDE.md` | No | Loaded only when you read/edit files in `frontend/` |
70:| `/mymonorepo/backend/CLAUDE.md` | No | Loaded only when you read/edit files in `backend/` |
71:| `/mymonorepo/api/CLAUDE.md` | No | Loaded only when you read/edit files in `api/` |
84:| `/mymonorepo/CLAUDE.md` | Yes | It's an ancestor directory |
85:| `/mymonorepo/frontend/CLAUDE.md` | Yes | It's your current working directory |
86:| `/mymonorepo/backend/CLAUDE.md` | No | Different branch of the directory tree |
87:| `/mymonorepo/api/CLAUDE.md` | No | Different branch of the directory tree |
91:1. **Ancestors always load at startup** — Claude walks UP the directory tree and loads all CLAUDE.md files it finds. This ensures you always have access to root-level, repository-wide instructions.
93:2. **Descendants load lazily** — Subdirectory CLAUDE.md files only load when you interact with files in those subdirectories. This prevents irrelevant context from bloating your session.
95:3. **Siblings never load** — If you're working in `frontend/`, you won't get `backend/CLAUDE.md` or `api/CLAUDE.md` loaded into context.
97:4. **Global CLAUDE.md** — You can also place a CLAUDE.md at `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` in your home folder, which applies to ALL Claude Code sessions regardless of project.
101:- **Shared instructions propagate down** — Root-level CLAUDE.md contains repository-wide conventions, coding standards, and common patterns that apply everywhere.
105:- **Context is optimized** — By lazily loading descendant CLAUDE.md files, Claude Code avoids loading potentially hundreds of kilobytes of irrelevant instructions at startup.
109:1. **Put shared conventions in root CLAUDE.md** — Coding standards, commit message formats, PR templates, and other repository-wide guidelines.
111:2. **Put component-specific instructions in component CLAUDE.md** — Framework-specific patterns, component architecture, testing conventions unique to that component.
120:- [Boris Cherny on X - Clarification on CLAUDE.md Loading](https://x.com/bcherny/status/2016339448863355206)

docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:255:**Rationale**: the manifest's `@latest` install commands ARE the SOTA-discipline contract per CR-6 fresh-from-github (do not pre-pin to stale versions in the planning document). The promotion-gate state machine above enforces this — a row cannot advance `STAGED → INSTALLED` until step 3 (resolved-version pin) lands in the manifest row.
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:257:**Scope**: applies to ALL install commands carrying raw `@latest` AND `@HEAD`-equivalent pulls (`git clone --depth 1 ... origin/main` without SHA pin). Does NOT apply to `@<version-tag>` pinned install commands (those satisfy CR-9 directly).

docs/install-provenance.md:4797:The plugin's `cli.bundle.mjs` ctx_doctor implementation tries `spawn('bun', ...)` to verify the Bun runtime is available. On Windows, Node's `child_process.spawn` doesn't auto-resolve `.cmd` shims unless `shell: true` — and `npm install -g bun` creates `bun.cmd` shim, not `bun.exe`. Solution: install via official bun.sh installer which produces real `bun.exe`.
